<file path="portal/src/lib/prism-project-resolver.ts">
/**
 * Prism Project Resolver
 * =======================
 *
 * Validates the `project` arg on every prism memory write by cross-
 * referencing the `prism_projects` registry against the `files_changed[]`
 * filesystem prefixes. Auto-creates project rows on first save when
 * a clear common prefix is present.
 *
 * Background:
 *   The 2026-04-30 prism-aac Azure-leak memory-loss bug was caused by
 *   the absence of this resolver — the prism-mcp client wrote under
 *   project="prism-mcp" instead of "prism-aac", and the entry became
 *   invisible to project-scoped searches.
 *
 * Contract:
 *   resolveProject(supabase, userId, declaredProject, filesChanged)
 *     → { ok: true, project }                  // accept the declared project
 *     → { ok: true, project, autoCreated }     // declared project was new, registry row added
 *     → { ok: false, status: 422, error, hint } // declared project conflicts with files_changed
 */
⋮----
import type { SupabaseClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
⋮----
export interface ResolveOk {
  ok: true;
  project: string;
  autoCreated?: boolean;
}
⋮----
export interface ResolveErr {
  ok: false;
  status: 422;
  error: string;
  hint?: string;
}
⋮----
export type ResolveResult = ResolveOk | ResolveErr;
⋮----
/**
 * Longest common prefix of an array of paths. Returns "" when no
 * meaningful prefix can be derived (single file, or the prefix is
 * shorter than a directory boundary).
 */
export function commonPathPrefix(paths: string[]): string
⋮----
// Single path: the candidate prefix is its directory, not the file itself.
// Without this, the resolver would treat e.g. `/etc/passwd` as a project root.
⋮----
/**
 * Returns true if `path` lies under `repoPath` (exact match or has
 * `repoPath/` prefix).
 */
function isUnder(path: string, repoPath: string): boolean
⋮----
interface ProjectRow {
  name: string;
  repo_path: string | null;
}
⋮----
/**
 * Picks the registered project whose repo_path is a prefix of every
 * file in `filesChanged`. Longest match wins so nested repos work.
 */
function pickFromRegistry(
  registry: ProjectRow[],
  filesChanged: string[]
): string | null
⋮----
export async function resolveProject(
  supabase: SupabaseClient,
  userId: string,
  declaredProject: string,
  filesChanged: string[] | undefined | null
): Promise<ResolveResult>
⋮----
// Without files_changed we cannot validate. Accept the declared
// project as-is (the resolver only blocks when there's evidence of
// a mismatch).
⋮----
// Case A: registry knows about a project that owns these files, and
// the agent declared something different → reject with corrective hint.
⋮----
// Case B: registry confirms the declared project — accept.
⋮----
// Case C: declared project is already registered (perhaps with no
// repo_path or with a repo_path that doesn't match these files) —
// accept without auto-creating.
⋮----
// Case D: declared project is brand new. If files_changed has a
// clear common prefix, auto-create the registry row so future
// writes are validated against it.
⋮----
// Non-fatal — accept the write, but the registry won't help next time.
⋮----
// Case E: brand-new project, no derivable prefix — accept with a
// soft warning so we still capture the write.
</file>

<file path="portal/src/lib/stripe.ts">
/**
 * Stripe Integration — Payments & Subscriptions
 * ================================================
 *
 * Stripe handles ALL personal and payment info:
 *   - Customer profiles (name, email, address)
 *   - Subscription management (standard → advanced → enterprise)
 *   - 14-day free trial on all paid tiers
 *   - Payment methods (cards, invoices)
 *   - Usage-based billing metering
 *
 * The portal stores ONLY the stripe_customer_id — no PCI data.

<file path="portal/src/app/api/v1/prism/memory/route.ts">
/**
 * Prism Cloud — Memory Sync Endpoint
 * =====================================
 *
 * Allows Prism CLI to sync session memory to Supabase via cloud proxy.
 * Standard+ tiers only — Free users use local SQLite.
 *
 * POST /api/v1/prism/memory
 * Body: { action: 'save_ledger' | 'save_handoff' | 'load_context' |
 *                 'search' | 'save_experience' | 'forget_memory', ...params }
 *
 * See portal/docs/PHASE_3_PORTAL_ENDPOINTS.md for the full action
 * catalog, rationale per action, and the rollout plan.
 */
⋮----
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { authenticateRequest } from '@/lib/auth-middleware';
import { checkAndIncrementPrismUsage, getTierLimits } from '@/lib/prism-limits';
import { withAudit } from '@/lib/audit-middleware';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/db';
import { resolveProject } from '@/lib/prism-project-resolver';
⋮----
// Actions whose payload includes `project`. forget_memory and
// health_check are the exceptions — forget_memory operates on a
// memory_id (project is a property of the row), and health_check
// is account-scoped.
⋮----
type MemoryAction = typeof VALID_ACTIONS[number];
⋮----
// Actions that DO NOT consume daily memory quota. Diagnostic pings
// must remain cheap so clients can health-check without burning quota.
⋮----
async function _POST(req: NextRequest)
⋮----
// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
⋮----
// Quota check after action validation so health_check (and any
// future diagnostic pings) can be excluded without burning quota.
⋮----
// event_type is NOT NULL on session_ledger with default
// 'session'. Omit the field when the caller didn't supply
// one so the DB default applies; explicitly setting null
// here violates the NOT NULL constraint and breaks save.
⋮----
// ─── save_handoff ────────────────────────────────────
// Routes through the save_handoff_with_version SQL RPC
// (same path direct-Supabase clients take). The RPC handles:
//   - column mapping (key_context/active_branch live in
//     handoff metadata; pending_todo is the canonical
//     column name even though the wire calls it open_todos)
//   - version increment + optimistic concurrency control
//   - default role / context_level NOT NULL fields
// OCC: pass expected_version to refuse blind overwrites of
// a concurrent session's writes (version mismatch → conflict).
⋮----
// ─── save_experience ─────────────────────────────────
// Structured behavioral event (correction / success / failure
// / learning / validation_result). Mirrors session_save_ledger
// but persists with event_type set so the agent's behavioral
// pattern detector can later mine corrections vs successes.
// Corrections start at importance=1 to jumpstart visibility
// in retrieval — the same convention as the prism-mcp client.
//
// Wire-protocol note: the experience's "what was tried" field
// is named `attempt` on the portal (not `action`) because
// `action` is reserved for the route dispatcher above. The
// prism-mcp tool schema names it `action`; clients translate.
⋮----
type ExperienceType = typeof VALID_EVENT_TYPES[number];
⋮----
// ─── forget_memory ───────────────────────────────────
// GDPR Article 17 compliance — surgical, single-entry deletion.
// Soft delete (default) sets deleted_at + deleted_reason and
// hides the row from search; hard delete physically removes
// the row (irreversible). Always scoped to the caller's user_id
// so a user can never delete another user's memory even with
// a guessed UUID.
⋮----
// Server-side UUID shape check so a malformed id doesn't
// become a 500 "invalid input syntax for type uuid" from
// Postgres. RFC 4122 (any version) — 8-4-4-4-12 hex.
⋮----
// ─── knowledge_search ────────────────────────────────
// Cross-project keyword + category search. Upgrade of
// `search` (which is project-scoped + summary-only): this
// returns full ledger fields, accepts a keywords[] array
// intersected against the row's keywords column, optional
// project and category filters, and an explicit limit cap.
// Tier-gated via the same memory quota as save_ledger.
⋮----
// ─── memory_history ──────────────────────────────────
// Time-travel timeline of handoff snapshots for a project.
// Reads session_handoffs_history (versioned upsert log).
// Used by the prism-mcp memory_history tool to find a
// target_version before memory_checkout.
⋮----
// ─── health_check ────────────────────────────────────
// Diagnostic ping. Returns the caller's daily usage row +
// recent activity summary. Useful as a thin-client liveness
// probe — confirms JWT is valid, prism_usage row exists,
// and the user is below quota. Does NOT run the full prism
// fsck (that requires direct DB access for orphan detection;
// out of scope for portal-mediated diagnostics).
⋮----
// ─── search_memory (semantic / pgvector) ─────────────
// Cosine similarity search over session_ledger.embedding.
// The portal does NOT generate embeddings — clients send a
// pre-computed 768-dim vector (Voyage / Gemini / their
// choice of provider). The portal calls the
// match_session_ledger SECURITY DEFINER RPC which scopes
// to user_id and filters tombstones server-side.
//
// Optional: project, role, similarity_threshold (default
// 0.7), limit (default 5, capped at 20). Returns rows
// sorted by similarity descending.
⋮----
// ─── export_memory (GDPR Article 20) ─────────────────
// Right to data portability. Returns a JSON envelope with
// the project's full ledger (active rows, including
// event_type/decisions/keywords) and the current handoff.
// Tombstones are excluded by default (deleted_at IS NULL)
// so soft-deleted entries don't reappear in exports —
// pass include_deleted=true to override (audit / legal).
// Streaming and signed-URL are deferred until response
// size becomes a problem; today's payloads fit comfortably
// in a single JSON response.
</file>

</files>
